| `update_zabbix_app_connection` | `PATCH /api/v1/app-connections/zabbix/{connectionId}` | Update the specified Zabbix Connection. |
| `update_zabbix_secret_sync` | `PATCH /api/v1/secret-syncs/zabbix/{syncId}` | Update the specified Zabbix Sync. |
| `verify_mac_with_kms_key` | `POST /api/v1/kms/keys/{keyId}/verify-mac` | Verify an HMAC (MAC) for data with a KMS key. |
| `verify_with_kms_key` | `POST /api/v1/kms/keys/{keyId}/verify` | Verify data signatures with a KMS key. |

## Errors

| Tag | Categories | Matchers |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `BadRequest` | BadRequestError | `{ { status = 400 } }` |
| `Forbidden` | AuthError | `{ { status = 403 } }` |
| `NotFound` | BadRequestError | `{ { status = 404 } }` |
| `UnprocessableEntity` | BadRequestError | `{ { status = 422 } }` |
